[0m$ [0mcd /Users/michelkerkmeester/worktrees/public/455-expansive-depth-integration && sed -n 1150,1240p .skilled/commands/deep/assets/deep-research.yaml
step_graph_convergence:
        action: "Run convergence.cjs once: it evaluates the coverage graph, persists the graph snapshot and computes the whole stop decision"
        dry_run_event_policy: preview-only
        bash: 'node .skilled/skills/system-deep-loop/runtime/scripts/convergence.cjs --spec-folder "{spec_folder}" --loop-type "research" --session-id "{config.lineage.sessionId}" --convergence-mode "{convergence_mode}" --depth-mode "{depth_mode}" --persist-snapshot --iteration "{current_iteration}" --stop-decision --config-path "{state_paths.config}" --state-log "{state_paths.state_log}" --registry-path "{state_paths.registry}" --iteration-count "{iteration_count}"'
        note: "The script is the only place the stop decision is computed. step_check_convergence branches on its stop_decision output."
        outputs:
          - graph_decision: "Tool decision value (CONTINUE | STOP_ALLOWED | STOP_BLOCKED), else null when unavailable"
          - graph_decision_json: "JSON-encoded graph decision string, else null"
          - graph_signals_json: "Tool signals object, else {}"
          - graph_blockers_json: "Tool blockers array, else []"
          - graph_trace_json: "Tool trace payload, else []"
          - graph_convergence_score: "signals.blendedScore when present, else the best available graph score, else 0"
          - stop_decision: "Tool stop_decision object: decision, stopReason, reasonDetail, weightedStopScore, signals, gates, blockedBy, graphDecision, inputs"
          - loop_counters_json: "Tool loop_counters_json object: stuckCount and consecutiveFailureCount, both rebuilt from the state log on this pass"
          - stuck_count: "loop_counters_json.stuckCount"
          - consecutive_failure_count: "loop_counters_json.consecutiveFailureCount"
        on_missing_counters: "If loop_counters_json is missing from the output, halt with stop reason error. Never bind a default counter. Run step_release_lock before the halt returns control."
        bookkeeping_log: '{"type":"event","event":"graph_convergence","mode":"research","run":{current_iteration},"decision":{graph_decision_json},"signals":{graph_signals_json},"blockers":{graph_blockers_json},"timestamp":"{ISO_8601_NOW}","sessionId":"{config.lineage.sessionId}","generation":{config.lineage.generation}}'

      step_check_convergence:
        action: "Branch on the stop decision that convergence.cjs computed in step_graph_convergence"
        algorithm: |
          The whole stop decision is computed by convergence.cjs from the loop config, the state log, the findings registry and the coverage graph. This step performs no arithmetic and never recomputes or overrides the result.
          The ratio signals leave out every iteration whose status is thought, error or timeout, and stuck_count is loop_counters_json.stuckCount.
          The script applies this fixed order, first match wins:
          1. if iteration_count >= max_iterations: STOP (maxIterationsReached). This terminal cap bypasses every guard and veto; failed guards are recorded in stop_decision.blockedBy as synthesis evidence.
          1a. After 3 consecutive failed iterations (status error or timeout), halt the loop and enter synthesis with stop reason error and partial findings.
          2. if stuck_count >= config.stuckThreshold: STUCK_RECOVERY
          3. if convergence_mode == "off": skip convergence stop candidates and continue.
          3a. if stop_policy == "max-iterations" AND iteration_count < max_iterations: the vote is telemetry only and the decision is CONTINUE.
          4. a STOP candidate exists when every question is answered (all_questions_answered) or the weighted vote passes (composite_converged).
          5. if iteration_count < effective_min_iterations: CONTINUE (minIterationsNotReached)
          6. a failed quality guard turns the candidate into CONTINUE (qualityGuardFailed)
          7. the graph decision settles the candidate: STOP_ALLOWED keeps STOP, STOP_BLOCKED gives BLOCKED, CONTINUE or a missing graph decision gives CONTINUE.
          Bind the outputs from stop_decision:
            decision = stop_decision.decision
            reason = stop_decision.stopReason when it is not null, else stop_decision.reasonDetail
            reason_detail = stop_decision.reasonDetail
            weighted_stop_score = stop_decision.weightedStopScore
            stop_blocked = (stop_decision.decision == "BLOCKED")
            blocked_by_json = stop_decision.blockedBy
            blocked_by_csv = the members of stop_decision.blockedBy joined with commas
            gate_convergence_pass = (stop_decision.graphDecision == "STOP_ALLOWED")
            gate_convergence_score = graph_convergence_score
            gate_key_question_coverage_pass = no blocker named questionCoverage
            gate_evidence_density_pass = no blocker named sourceDiversity or evidenceDepth
            gate_evidence_density_sources = graph_signals_json.sourceDiversity or 0
            gate_hotspot_saturation_pass = true
            recovery_strategy = first blocker reason/message, else "Collect deeper graph evidence before stopping."
            min_iterations_guard_blocked = (stop_decision.reasonDetail == "minIterationsNotReached")
            min_iterations_guard_pass = true when effective_min_iterations > 1 and iteration_count >= effective_min_iterations and min_iterations_guard_already_passed == false
          If stop_decision is missing from the step_graph_convergence output, do not decide by hand: halt and report the script output.
          When stop_decision.reasonDetail == "qualityGuardFailed", log: "Quality guard failed: {blocked_by_csv}. Continuing."
          Segment-aware convergence is experimental/reference-only; live workflow uses the current session as a single segment.
        outputs:
          - decision: "STOP | BLOCKED | STUCK_RECOVERY | CONTINUE"
          - reason: "stop_decision.stopReason when set, else stop_decision.reasonDetail"
          - reason_detail: "stop_decision.reasonDetail, the fine cause behind the decision"
          - weighted_stop_score: "stop_decision.weightedStopScore, for diagnostics only"
          - stop_blocked: "Boolean true when stop_decision.decision == BLOCKED"
          - blocked_by_json: "JSON array of graph blocker names when decision == BLOCKED"
          - blocked_by_csv: "Comma-separated graph blocker names when decision == BLOCKED"
          - gate_convergence_pass: "Boolean pass/fail for the graph-assisted convergence gate"
          - gate_convergence_score: "Numeric score recorded from graph convergence"
          - gate_key_question_coverage_pass: "Boolean pass/fail derived from graph blocker coverage signals"
          - gate_evidence_density_pass: "Boolean pass/fail derived from graph blocker evidence-depth signals"
          - gate_evidence_density_sources: "Source-diversity count or equivalent evidence-depth proxy"
          - gate_hotspot_saturation_pass: "Research placeholder gate; true unless an explicit blocker sets otherwise"
          - recovery_strategy: "One-line hint describing the next research action when decision == BLOCKED or STUCK_RECOVERY"
          - min_iterations_guard_pass: "Boolean true when the configured convergence floor has cleared for the first time"
          - min_iterations_guard_blocked: "Boolean true when the configured convergence floor overrides a STOP candidate to CONTINUE"

      step_emit_min_iterations_guard_pass:
        action: "Persist a first-class event when the convergence floor clears"
        if_pass:
          dry_run_event_policy: preview-only
          append_jsonl: '{"type":"event","event":"min_iterations_guard_pass","mode":"research","run":{current_iteration},"iterationCount":{iteration_count},"minIterations":{effective_min_iterations},"timestamp":"{ISO_8601_NOW}","sessionId":"{config.lineage.sessionId}","generation":{config.lineage.generation}}'
          log: "Minimum iteration floor cleared for convergence stopping."

      step_emit_blocked_stop:

        if_blocked:
          dry_run_event_policy: preview-only
          append_to_jsonl: '{"type":"event","event":"blocked_stop","mode":"research","run":{current_iteration},"blockedBy":{blocked_by_json},"gateResults":{"convergence":{"pass":{gate_convergence_pass},"score":{gate_convergence_score}},"keyQuestionCoverage":{"pass":{gate_key_question_coverage_pass},"answered":{answered_count},"total":{total_questions}},"evidenceDensity":{"pass":{gate_evidence_density_pass},"sources":{gate_evidence_density_sources}},"hotspotSaturation":{"pass":{gate_hotspot_saturation_pass}}},"recoveryStrategy":"{recovery_strategy}","timestamp":"{ISO_8601_NOW}","sessionId":"{config.lineage.sessionId}","generation":{config.lineage.generation}}'
          log: "Graph convergence blocked STOP. Failed blockers: {blocked_by_csv}. Recovery strategy: {recovery_strategy}"
          set:
            decision: "CONTINUE"
            reason: "blockedStop"
            stop_blocked: false

      step_handle_convergence:

---
title: "Review minIterations floor"
description: "A review run with a configured minIterations floor returns CONTINUE with minIterationsNotReached until its iteration count reaches the floor, so a review never stops before the floor."
trigger_phrases:

  - "minIterationsNotReached"
  - "review iteration floor"
  - "antiConvergence minIterations review"
version: 1.0.1.0

# Review minIterations floor

## 1. OVERVIEW

The review convergence check holds a stop below the configured floor. `antiConvergence.minIterations` in the review config sets the floor, and the shipped default is 2. The floor applies to research and review alike.

---

## 2. HOW IT WORKS

### Floor Check

`runtime/scripts/convergence.cjs` returns `CONTINUE` with the reason `minIterationsNotReached` while the iteration count is below the floor. A stop candidate below the floor never ends the review.

### Stop Order

The review command's `step_check_convergence` places the floor in the fixed stop order after the candidate line and before the gates, so the floor is checked before any gate can end the run.

---

## 3. SOURCE FILES

### Implementation

| File | Layer | Role |
|---|---|---|
| `.skilled/skills/system-deep-loop/runtime/scripts/convergence.cjs` | Script | The floor check that returns `CONTINUE` with `minIterationsNotReached`. |
| `.skilled/commands/deep/assets/deep-review.yaml` | Command workflow | The floor's position in the fixed stop order of `step_check_convergence`. |
| `.skilled/skills/system-deep-loop/deep-review/assets/deep-review-config.json` | Config | `antiConvergence.minIterations`, default 2. |

### Validation And Tests

| File | Type | Role |
|---|---|---|
| `.skilled/skills/system-deep-loop/runtime/tests/unit/stop-decision.vitest.ts` | Vitest | The review floor case: `CONTINUE` with `minIterationsNotReached`. |
| `.skilled/skills/system-deep-loop/runtime/tests/integration/review-depth-convergence.vitest.ts` | Vitest | A configured floor above 2. |
| `.skilled/skills/system-deep-loop/runtime/tests/integration/review-stop-path-replay.vitest.ts` | Vitest | The shipped floor replays unchanged. |
| `.skilled/skills/system-deep-loop/deep-review/manual-testing-playbook/convergence-and-recovery/review-min-iterations-floor.md` | Manual playbook | DRV-072: a review run holds below its minIterations floor. |

---

<!-- SPECKIT_TEMPLATE_SOURCE: spec-core | v2.4 -->
# Feature Specification: Expansive-depth sub-mode for deep-research and deep-review

<!-- SPECKIT_LEVEL: 1 -->
---

<!-- ANCHOR:metadata -->
## 1. METADATA

| Field | Value |
|-------|-------|
| **Level** | 1 |
| **Priority** | P1 |
| **Status** | In Progress |
| **Created** | 2026-11-17 |
| **Branch** | `main` |
<!-- /ANCHOR:metadata -->

---

<!-- ANCHOR:problem -->
## 2. PROBLEM & PURPOSE

### Problem Statement
The deep-research and deep-review loops converge on the three to five questions seeded at init and stop once novelty drops, so a broad target such as "research the last 100 commits for bugs, drift and improvements" or "analyze system-skill-advisor for chat-time auto-utilization" gets a narrow, shallow answer. Nothing in the loop adds scope as it learns.

### Purpose
Produce a research-backed design for an additive, opt-in expansive-depth sub-mode that widens and deepens a run's scope, grounded in the current convergence and focus code and in the 002-graph-engineering program, then build it with the extension and defect fixes through phases 001-014.
<!-- /ANCHOR:problem -->

<!-- ANCHOR:measured-behavior -->
### Measured Behavior
```text
N/A: this packet is research only. The deep-research loop in research/ measures the current stop and focus behavior from source and records it in research/research.md.
```
<!-- /ANCHOR:measured-behavior -->

---

<!-- ANCHOR:scope -->
## 3. SCOPE

<!-- DR-SEED:SCOPE -->
### In Scope
- Diagnose why the current loops stop early or stay narrow: convergence.cjs stop decision, newInfoRatio, minIterations, focus selection, strategy Next Focus, coverage graph, divergent mode and the wave_orchestration appendix.
- Mine the 002-graph-engineering program (children 001-006, research/, reference/, context/) for usable graph-loop principles.
- Design the expansive-depth sub-mode and its additive integration into agents, commands, workflow YAMLs, mode registry and convergence logic.
- Record the dispatch-iteration.cjs config-dir routing defect for research runs as a finding.
- Build what the research designed, through phases 001-014 in the Phase Documentation Map: the completed-run extension (001), the defect fixes D1-D14 that the research kept (002-009, 012, 013, the dispatch-iteration.cjs config-dir defect among them in 002), the opt-in expansive-depth sub-mode for deep-research (010) and deep-review (011), and the playbook coverage, feature catalogs and alignment pass (014).

### Out of Scope

# Goal: Opt-in expansive depth mode for deep-review

<!-- SPECKIT_TEMPLATE_SOURCE: goal | v2.2 -->
<!-- HVR_REFERENCE: .skilled/skills/sk-doc/sk-create-with-human-voice/references/hvr-rules.md -->
<!-- GOAL_AUTHORING: .skilled/skills/sk-doc/sk-create-goal/SKILL.md -->

---

<!-- ANCHOR:directive -->
## 1. DURABLE DIRECTIVE
<!-- FILL: Write one sentence: what the packet is for, not how. GOOD: Keep only the newest n archives on request. WEAK: Improve the script. -->

**Objective:** With `--depth-mode=expansive`, a deep-review run decomposes its target into admitted sub-target nodes held in `candidateCoverage`, `searchDebt` and `registry.frontier`, focuses and closes them breadth-first and partitions depth-1 nodes across fan-out lineages, and without the flag nothing changes.

### Decisions

Frozen choices. Changing one is an amendment.

| ID | Decision |
|----|----------|
| D1 | The mode is opt-in: with `depthMode` absent or `--depth-mode=default`, every review output is byte-identical to the pre-phase baseline. |
| D2 | Review admission uses the same six ordered checks as the research frontier and writes into `searchDebt` and `registry.frontier`; review never writes an inbox row. |
| D3 | Under the flag the effective review focus precedence is blocked-stop text, pivot focus, frontier node, leaf `nextFocus`, first uncovered dimension. |
| D4 | The pivot boundary regexes and the configured-target ban do not change. |

<!-- /ANCHOR:directive -->

---

[0m$ [0mcd /Users/michelkerkmeester/worktrees/public/455-expansive-depth-integration && sed -n 1,120p .skilled/skills/system-deep-loop/runtime/scripts/review-verdict.cjs
#!/usr/bin/env node

// ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// MODULE: Deep-Loop Review Verdict
// ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

// The verdict a review stops on is a function of the state it stopped in, never of
// the wording a report chose: a remaining P0, or any required quality gate that
// failed at the terminal stop, is a FAIL; a remaining P1, or a search-debt
// obligation the run never covered, is a CONDITIONAL; and anything else is a PASS
// that still records whether P2 advisories remain. The merged verdict and the
// per-lineage attribution verdict both read the rule from here, so one stop cannot
// be a failure in one table and a pass in another.

'use strict';

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// 1. IMPORTS
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

const {
  assertKnownFlags,
} = require('./lib/cli-guards.cjs');

const {
  readJsonObjectFile,
  readStateLogRows,
} = require('./convergence.cjs');

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// 2. CONSTANTS
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

// Every key this entrypoint reads from the parsed arguments. A flag missing here
// is treated as an unknown option, so this list is the accepted set.
const REVIEW_VERDICT_FLAGS = ['registryPath', 'stateLog', 'activeP0', 'activeP1', 'activeP2', 'searchDebt', 'failedGateIds'];
// Any one of these flags means the caller already bound the counts in a merge, and
// the bound values are then the authority: re-reading the files could bind a state
// the merge never decided on.
const BOUND_VALUE_FLAGS = ['activeP0', 'activeP1', 'activeP2', 'searchDebt', 'failedGateIds'];
// A verdict is a report, not a mutation, so a FAIL is still a successful run and an
// unreadable input is the only condition that stops the script.
const EXIT_ERROR = 2;

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// 3. HELPERS
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

function inputError(message) {
  const err = new Error(message);
  err.code = 'INPUT_VALIDATION';
  return err;
}

function jsonOut(payload) {
  process.stdout.write(`${JSON.stringify(payload)}\n`);
}

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// 4. CORE LOGIC
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

function isActiveFinding(finding) {
  return (finding?.disposition ?? finding?.status) === 'active';
}

// A per-lineage verdict must apply the same active-disposition guard an active
// count uses, not the registry's raw findingsBySeverity — a raw count can include
// findings that were since resolved, which would let one report's verdict diverge
// from another's over the same findings.
function activeReviewSeverityCounts(registry) {
  const openFindings = Array.isArray(registry?.openFindings) ? registry.openFindings : [];
  const active = openFindings.filter(isActiveFinding);
  return {
    P0: active.filter((finding) => finding?.severity === 'P0').length,
    P1: active.filter((finding) => finding?.severity === 'P1').length,
    P2: active.filter((finding) => finding?.severity === 'P2').length,
  };
}

// Open search debt is one obligation per bug class the search path still owes. A
// registry written before the ledger existed carries no array, which owes nothing
// rather than an unknown amount.
function openSearchDebtCount(registry) {
  return Array.isArray(registry?.searchDebt) ? registry.searchDebt.length : 0;
}

// One shape for the ids a report prints, whatever order and however many times a
// caller bound them.
function normalizeGateIds(ids) {
  const values = Array.isArray(ids) ? ids : [];
  return [...new Set(values.map((id) => String(id)).filter(Boolean))].sort();
}

// The state log is the first source: the reducer can run before the terminal-gates
// row is written, so a registry can predate it. A required gate the lineage still
// failed is a stop no verdict may read as a pass.
function lineageFailedRequiredGateIds({ registry, stateRecords }) {
  const latest = (Array.isArray(stateRecords) ? stateRecords : [])
    .filter((record) => record?.type === 'event' && record?.event === 'terminal_gates')
    .at(-1);
  const ids = latest ? latest.failedRequiredGateIds : registry?.terminalGates?.failedRequiredGateIds;
  return normalizeGateIds(ids);
}

function deriveReviewVerdict({
  activeP0 = 0,
  activeP1 = 0,
  activeP2 = 0,
  openSearchDebt = 0,
  failedRequiredGateIds = [],
} = {}) {
  const failedGateIds = Array.isArray(failedRequiredGateIds) ? failedRequiredGateIds : [];
  let verdict = 'PASS';
  if (activeP0 > 0 || failedGateIds.length > 0) verdict = 'FAIL';
  else if (activeP1 > 0 || openSearchDebt > 0) verdict = 'CONDITIONAL';
  // Advisories mark a pass that carried suggestions; they never lift the verdict
  // above PASS and never change one.
  return { verdict, hasAdvisories: verdict === 'PASS' && activeP2 > 0 };
}
> build · deepseek-v4.1-flash

+      step_extend_completed_run:
+        action: "Extend a completed run in place, under the lock and before classification, so the classifier sees a resumable run"
+        skip_when: "lineage_mode != 'completed-continue'"
+        dry_run_boundary: "state-mutation"
+        note: |
+          --extend=N continues a finished run in the same session and generation. The
+          duration clock counts wall-clock time since createdAt, idle time included, so the
+          new budget starts from whichever is later, the old budget or the time already
+          spent, and createdAt is never re-stamped. The event is appended before the
+          config is replaced, so a failed append leaves the run complete and untouched.
+        command: |
+          GATEWAY=".skilled/skills/system-deep-loop/runtime/scripts/append-mode-event.cjs"
+          [ -f "$GATEWAY" ] || GATEWAY="scripts/append-mode-event.cjs"
+          WORK_DIR="$(mktemp -d "${TMPDIR:-/tmp}/deep-review-extend.XXXXXX")"
+          node -e '
+            const fs = require("fs"), crypto = require("crypto"), path = require("path");
+            const [configPath, stateLogPath, extendRaw, extendMinutesRaw, workDir] = process.argv.slice(1);
+            const fail = (message) => { console.error(message); process.exit(2); };
+            const config = JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(configPath, "utf8"));
+            if (config.status !== "complete") {
+              fail("--extend needs a completed run, and this run has status " + config.status + ". Use --lineage-mode=resume to continue an unfinished run.");
+            }
+            const stateBytes = fs.readFileSync(stateLogPath);
+            const rows = stateBytes.toString("utf8").split("\n").filter(Boolean).flatMap((line) => {
+              try { return [JSON.parse(line)]; } catch { return []; }
+            });
+            // One run per iteration number, error and timeout rows included.
+            const continuedFromRun = new Set(rows
+              .filter((row) => row && row.type === "iteration")
+              .map((row) => row.run ?? row.iteration)).size;
+            const addedIterations = Number(extendRaw);
+            const priorMaxIterations = Number(config.maxIterations) || 0;
+            const priorMaxDurationMinutes = Number(config.maxDurationMinutes) || 0;
+            const createdMs = Date.parse(config.createdAt);
+            const elapsedMinutesAtExtension = Number.isFinite(createdMs)
+              ? Math.max(0, Math.floor((Date.now() - createdMs) / 60000))
+              : 0;
+            // An unset flag renders as an empty or unexpanded value, never as digits.
+            const hasMinutes = /^[1-9][0-9]*$/.test(String(extendMinutesRaw));
+            const addedMinutes = hasMinutes ? Number(extendMinutesRaw) : priorMaxDurationMinutes;
+            // A run with no duration budget keeps none unless --extend-minutes asks for one.
+            const newMaxDurationMinutes = priorMaxDurationMinutes > 0 || hasMinutes
+              ? Math.max(priorMaxDurationMinutes, elapsedMinutesAtExtension) + addedMinutes
+              : priorMaxDurationMinutes;
+            const newMaxIterations = continuedFromRun + addedIterations;
+            const sessionId = config.sessionId;
+            const generation = Number(config.generation) || 1;
+            const event = {
+              stem: "deep_review.run_extended",
+              scope: { runId: sessionId, sessionId, generation },
+              data: {
+                priorTailDigest: crypto.createHash("sha256").update(stateBytes).digest("hex"),
+                continuedFromRun,
+                extension: {
+                  addedIterations,
+                  priorMaxIterations,
+                  newMaxIterations,
+                  priorMaxDurationMinutes,
+                  newMaxDurationMinutes,
+                  elapsedMinutesAtExtension,
+                },
+              },
+            };
+            const next = {
+              ...config,
+              status: "initialized",
+              maxIterations: newMaxIterations,
+              maxDurationMinutes: newMaxDurationMinutes,
+              lineageMode: "completed-continue",
+              continuedFromRun,
+            };
+            fs.writeFileSync(path.join(workDir, "event.json"), JSON.stringify(event));
+            fs.writeFileSync(path.join(workDir, "config.json"), JSON.stringify(next, null, 2) + "\n");
+            const stopPolicy = config.antiConvergence && config.antiConvergence.stopPolicy || "convergence";
+            // Review has no question inbox, so under convergence an unchanged dimension set
+            // can re-stop on all_dimensions_clean before the new budget is used.
+            const warning = stopPolicy === "convergence"
+              ? "This run stops on convergence, so it may re-stop on all_dimensions_clean right away. Pass --convergence-mode=off or use stopPolicy max-iterations to keep it running."
+              : null;
+            console.log(JSON.stringify({ continuedFromRun, newMaxIterations, newMaxDurationMinutes, warning }));
+          ' "{state_paths.config}" "{state_paths.state_log}" "{extend}" "{extend_minutes}" "$WORK_DIR" > "$WORK_DIR/result.json" || { rm -rf "$WORK_DIR"; exit 2; }
+          node "$GATEWAY" --mode review --run-directory "$(dirname "{state_paths.state_log}")" --event-json "$WORK_DIR/event.json" || { rm -rf "$WORK_DIR"; exit 1; }
+          mv "$WORK_DIR/config.json" "{state_paths.config}" || { rm -rf "$WORK_DIR"; exit 1; }
+          cat "$WORK_DIR/result.json"
+          rm -rf "$WORK_DIR"
+        outputs:
+          - continued_from_run: "continuedFromRun from the JSON output"
+          - max_iterations: "newMaxIterations from the JSON output; it replaces the command default so the loop cap and the convergence script read the same ceiling"
+          - extension_pending: "true"
+          - extension_warning: "warning from the JSON output, null when none"
+        on_warning: "When extension_warning is not null, print it and continue. It never refuses the extension."
+        on_fail: "Exit 2 is a usage error, such as a run that is not complete: halt with that message through step_release_lock and leave the config untouched. Any other failure halts with STATUS=FAIL through step_release_lock."
+
+      step_snapshot_extended_review_report:

[0m$ [0mcd /Users/michelkerkmeester/worktrees/public/455-expansive-depth-integration && sed -n 620,635p .skilled/skills/system-deep-loop/deep-review/references/protocol/loop-protocol.md
#### Completed-run extension

`--extend=N` (and optionally `--extend-minutes=M`) continues a run whose config has `status: "complete"`. Under the loop lock and before session classification, `step_extend_completed_run`:

1. Counts `continuedFromRun` as the distinct runs among the iteration records, error and timeout rows included.
2. Sets `maxIterations` to `continuedFromRun + N`, so a run stopped at 7 of 10 and extended by 3 ends at 10.
3. Sets `maxDurationMinutes` to `max(prior budget, minutes elapsed since createdAt) + M`. `M` defaults to the prior budget, and a run with no duration budget keeps none unless `--extend-minutes` is passed. `createdAt` is never re-stamped, because the duration check counts wall-clock time, idle time included.
4. Appends `deep_review.run_extended` through the append gateway, which projects an `extended` row into the state log, and only then replaces the config with `status: "initialized"`, `lineageMode: "completed-continue"` and `continuedFromRun`. A failed append leaves the run complete and untouched.

Classification then lands on resume. The workflow writes no `resumed` row for an extension, and the reducers treat an `extended` row like `resumed`, so the run is live again. Before the first new dispatch, `step_snapshot_extended_review_report` copies the prior review report to `review/review-report-v{generation}-r{continuedFromRun}.md` and refuses to overwrite an existing snapshot, so a second extension of the same generation keeps its own copy. `--extend` refuses a run that is not complete (use `--lineage-mode=resume`), refuses fan-out before any lineage spawns, and accepts positive integers only.

The extended row carries `{"type":"event","event":"extended","sessionId","generation","lineageMode":"completed-continue","continuedFromRun","extension","timestamp"}`, where `extension` holds `addedIterations`, `priorMaxIterations`, `newMaxIterations`, `priorMaxDurationMinutes`, `newMaxDurationMinutes` and `elapsedMinutesAtExtension`. It has no `archivedPath` or `parentSessionId`, so the field rule below covers `resumed` and `restarted` only.

**Convergence window.** Under `stopPolicy: convergence`, an extension with nothing new to cover re-stops on `all_dimensions_clean` at the first convergence check. The extension step prints a warning when this is likely; to keep the run going, pass `--convergence-mode=off` or use `stopPolicy: max-iterations`.

**Consecutive failures.** A run that completed on consecutive failures re-stops at the consecutive-failure check even under `--convergence-mode=off`, because that check runs before the `off` branch and the trailing failed iterations are still in the log.
